## `AFG-4620-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Ear-Tag-Traceability Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Imported Grain GMO Strip Screening & Livestock Foot-and-Mouth Quarantine
